Compute C via FFT:
```
def cross_corr_full_fft(h):
    n=len(h)
    N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)  # for c only need 2n-1
    H=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    G=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)
    I=np.fft.irfft(H*np.conj(G),N)
    # I length N real
    neg = I[n-1:0:-1] # length n: neg n-1 down to 0? Wait I[n-1] neg n-1, ... I[1] neg1, I[0] pos0
    pos = I[N-1:N-n:-1] # pos1...pos n-1
    return dx*np.concatenate((neg,pos))
```
Let's test n=3,N=8: I[2]=neg2,I[1]=neg1,I[0]=pos0,I[7]=pos1,I[6]=pos2. `I[n-1:0:-1]` = I[2],I[1],I[0]. `I[N-1:N-n:-1]` = I[7],I[6] (stop N-n=5 exclusive, step -1 -> 7,6). Good. length 5. Multiply dx. Need numerical imag small, take real (irfft returns real). But due round, maybe do `I = (I + I[::-1]?` no).

Our c order does exactly d=-(n-1) to n-1. So w_ext = w_full. Good.

Then compute S1 (∑ w_{m-j}h_m) = conv(w_rev, h)[c:c+n] where w_rev = w_ext[::-1]? Need w_rev index p=2n-2-e: w_rev[p]=w_ext[2n-2-p] -> d_rev = d_ext -? If e=c+d, p=2n-2-c-d = c-d, corresponding d' = p-c = -d. So yes reverse.

In fun_and_grad:
```
x = np.asarray(x) # no copy
# maybe clip for safety? If x in bounds, no.
H = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
ones_fft = np.fft.rfft(np.ones(n, dtype=float), N) # precompute to avoid each time. But passing target.
G = ones_fft - H
I = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(G), N)
c = (I[n-1:0:-1] and I[N-1:N-n:-1]) * dx
# ensure real, maybe take .real
cmax = c.max()
v = alpha*(c-cmax)
# For stability: v <=0, max 0; but due cmax maybe not exact? vmax=0 if cmax max. use.
w = np.exp(v)  # v could underflow to 0 for most, ok
wsum = w.sum()
if wsum ==0: w... set max? But cmax finite, v=0 for at least one, exp(0)=1, so sum>0.
f = cmax + np.log(wsum)/alpha
# penalty
s = x.sum()
diff = s - target
f += 0.5*mu*diff*diff
# weights w normalized
w = w / wsum
# compute S1,S2
# w_ext = w (order d -n+1..n-1)
# Fw = rfft(w,N)
Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
# derive Fw_rev using formula to save? Let's implement with care.
L = 2*n-1
# For rfft indices k=0..N/2. W_rev[k] = exp(-2πi k (L-1)/N) * conj(Fw[k])
# For real rfft, we can compute complex factor.
phase = np.exp(-2j*np.pi*np.arange(Fw.size)*(L-1)/N)
Fw_rev = phase * np.conj(Fw)
# But for k=N/2 phase = exp(-2πi (N/2)(L-1)/N) = exp(-πi(L-1)) = 1 because L-1 even. conj same. ok.
S1 = np.fft.irfft(Fw_rev * H, N)[c_idx:c_idx+n]
S2 = np.fft.irfft(Fw * H, N)[c_idx:c_idx+n]
# A via prefix of w:
# starts = np.arange(c, -1, -1), ends=starts+n-1; prefix sum w
pref = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
starts = np.arange(c, -1, -1, dtype=int)
A = pref[starts+n] - pref[starts] # since end inclusive start+n-1 => pref[start+n]-pref[start]
# grad = dx*(A - S1 - S2)
grad = dx*(A - S1 - S2)
# penalty gradient
grad += mu*diff  # because 0.5 derivative diff*n? μ*diff*ones (since d sum/dx_i=1). yes.
return f, grad
```

FFT_N(w_rev_ext) at k: ∑_{p=0}^{L-1} w[L-1-p] e^{-2πi k p/N} = e^{-2πi k(L-1)/N} ∑_{q=0}^{L-1} w[q] e^{2πi k q/N}. Since w real, ∑ w[q] e^{+2πi kq/N}=conj(∑ w[q] e^{-...}) = conj(W[k]). Yes. def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2

Alternative robust: use binary search on level for box projection with sum constraint (Dinkelbach). We need project onto box and sum target. This is classic: find τ such that sum(clip(h-τ,0,1))=target. Use binary search O(n log precision). More reliable than iterative. Let's implement.

Projection onto {0≤h≤1, sum=target}:
We need find shift τ (real) such that p_i=clip(h_i - τ,0,1) sum=target. p monotone decreasing in τ. τ range [min(h)-? max?]. If target equals clipped sum, maybe.
Algorithm:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    if target <=0 or target>=n: return h
    # binary search tau
    lo = h.min() - 1.0 - 1e-12 # p all 1? if tau < min, p=clip(h-tau)?> maybe all 1 if h-tau≥1. Better lower = -1 - max? For sum target mid, tau within [min-1, max+1]
    hi = h.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
       p=np.clip(h - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
       s=p.sum()
       if s > target: # need larger tau
          lo=tau
       else:
          hi=tau
    tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
    p=np.clip(h-tau,0,1)
    diff=target-p.sum()
    # adjust among not at bounds
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       mask=(p>1e-15)&(p<1-1e-15)
       if mask.any(): p[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
       else:
          if diff>0: p[np.argsort(1-p)[:int(abs(diff))]] += ...? target integer, bounds not issue normally.
    return np.clip(p,0,1)
```
Binary search 80 iterations * n=2048 ~160k operations, fine. Values h may already near feasible; this projection may change shape significantly if sum off. But if L-BFGS with small penalty, sum off small. Good.

But for L-BFGS, if we project only after each alpha, gradient not aware. OK.

```
def shift_grad_single(h,n, idx):
    dx=2.0/n
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    k=idx-(n-1)
    if k>=0:
       nv=n-k
       grad[:nv] += (1-h[k:])*dx
       grad[k:] += (-h[:nv])*dx
    else:
       kk=-k; nv=n-kk
       grad[kk:] += (1-h[:nv])*dx
       grad[:nv] += (-h[kk:])*dx
```

def build_fft_context(n):
    N = 1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
    ones = np.ones(n, float)
    ones_fft = np.fft.rfft(ones, N)
    L=2*n-1
    phase = np.exp(-2j*np.pi*np.arange(ones_fft.size)*(L-1)/N)
    starts = np.arange(n-1, -1, -1, dtype=np.int64)

def sqp_optimize(h,n,ctx,deadline,max_iter=100,rho=0.02,max_active=40,exact_eval=True):
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      c=c_vec_fft(h,...)
      cmax=c.max(); active_idx=np.where(c >= cmax - max(1e-8, 1e-4*cmax))[0]
      if active_idx.size>max_active:
         # sort descending
         order=np.argsort(-c[active_idx]); active_idx=active_idx[order[:max_active]]
      # build LP
      nvar=n+1; c_obj=zeros; c_obj[-1]=1
      A_ub=zeros((len(active)+4*n,nvar)); b_ub=zeros(...)
      row=0
      for idx in active_idx:
          g=shift_grad_single(h,n,idx)
          A_ub[row,:n]=g
          A_ub[row,n]=-1
          b_ub[row]=-c[idx]
          row+=1
      # step bounds
      for i in range(n): A_ub[row,i]=-1; b_ub[row]=rho; row++
      for i: A_ub[row,i]=1; b_ub[row]=rho
      A_eq=zeros((1,nvar)); A_eq[0,:n]=1; b_eq=[0]
      bounds=[(None,None)]*n+[(0.0,2.0)]
      res=linprog(...)
      if not success: rho*=0.5; if rho<1e-6 break; continue
      delta=res.x[:n]
      # try scales
      accepted=False
      for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
          h_try=np.clip(h+scale*delta,0,1)
          # enforce exact sum before eval? project_feasible
          h_try=project_feasible(h_try,n)
          c_try=exact_c5(h_try,n) if n<=1200 else c_vec_fft max? Use exact for n<=1500, else fft for speed.
          if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
             best_c=c_try; best_h=h_try.copy(); h=h_try; accepted=True
             if c_try < best_c - 1e-7? maybe increase rho
             if scale==1: rho=min(rho*1.2,0.1)
             break
      if not accepted:
          rho*=0.5
          if rho<1e-7: break
          if no_accept_count>15: break
      # maybe if best_c lower than target early, continue? no
   return best_h,best_c
```
LP building with Python loops for n=1024 constraints 4000: okay? A_ub zeros then loops 4000, fine. Could vectorize for speed:
- Set step bounds using slice:

```

  if budget_s < 60: minimal
  n0=len(initial)

Implementation:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5*n
    h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    curr = h.sum()
    if abs(curr-target) < 1e-13:
       return h
    lo = h.min() - 1.0 - 1e-9
    hi = h.max() + 1.0 + 1e-9
    # ensure at lo sum >= target? if tau very low, p=1 for many? If h has some zeros and tau=-2, h - tau >1 -> clip1 all ones, sum=n>=target. If tau high, sum=0.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       p=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0)
       s=p.sum()
       if s > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
    p=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-p.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       mask=(p>1e-10)&(p<1-1e-10)
       if mask.any():
          p[mask] += diff/mask.sum()
       else:
          # integer target, adjust by rounding
          if diff>0:
             idx=np.argsort(1.0-p)[:max(1,int(abs(diff))+1)]
             for i in idx:
                add=min(1-p[i], max(0,diff))
                p[i]+=add; diff-=add
                if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
          else:
             idx=np.argsort(p)[:max(1,int(-diff)+1)]
             for i in idx:
                sub=min(p[i], -diff)
                p[i]-=sub; diff+=sub
                if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    return np.clip(p,0.0,1.0)
```
But if h initially sum too high, binary search with lo/hi as above works? If target less than current, need tau positive maybe hi high. At hi=max+1, h-tau ≤0 (except if h=max? max-(max+1)=-1) sum 0 <=target. At lo=min-1, h-tau ≥? min - (min-1)=1 => p=1 all, sum n >= target. So bracket. Good.
60 iterations enough double precision. O(60n). In L-BFGS after each alpha only few. In SQP, after each scale many: 60*n each, for n=1024 and 100 scales could be 6M operations, okay but maybe overhead. We can optimize projection for small diff: if abs(curr-target)<1e-8, use simple distribute instead of binary. Add:

if abs(curr-target) < 1e-7:
    diff=target-curr
    if diff>0:
        mask=h<1-1e-10
        if mask.any(): h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
    else:
        mask=h>1e-10; h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
    return clip
```

def smooth_perturb(h, n, seed, amp):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
    # low-pass via fft
    f=np.fft.rfft(noise,n)
    k=np.arange(len(f))
    f *= np.exp(-0.005*k**2) # adapt
    noise=np.fft.irfft(f,n)
    h2=h + amp*noise
    h2=project_feasible(h2,n)
    return h2
```

`build_context(n)`:
```
def build_context(n):
    N = 1 << (3*n - 2 - 1).bit_length()
    ones_fft = np.fft.rfft(np.ones(n, dtype=float), N)
    L = 2*n - 1
    phase = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * np.arange(ones_fft.size, dtype=float) * (L - 1) / float(N))
    starts = np.arange(n-1, -1, -1, dtype=np.intp)
    dx = 2.0/n
    target = 0.5*n
    return (n,N,ones_fft,phase,starts,dx,target)
```
For n=1? not. `3*n-2` maybe for n=500 =1498, bit_length of 1497? Use m=3*n-2. `N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()` assumes m>0. Good.

`c_vec_fft(h,ctx)` uses n,N,ones_fft,dx.

```
def c_vec_fft(h, ctx):
    n,N,ones_fft,phase,starts,dx,target = ctx
    H=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    G=ones_fft-H
    I=np.fft.irfft(H*np.conj(G),N)
    c=np.empty(2*n-1, dtype=float)
    c[:n]=I[n-1:0:-1]
    c[n:]=I[N-1:N-n:-1]
    c *= dx
    return c
```
Potential: `I[n-1:0:-1]` for n=1? n>1. For n=500 fine.

`c5_fft(h,ctx) = c_vec_fft(h,ctx).max()`.

`smooth_fun_and_grad`:
```
H = np.fft.rfft(x,N)
G = ones_fft - H
I = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(G), N)
c = np.empty(2*n-1); c[:n]=...; c*=dx
# numeric clip small negatives? 
cmax = c.max()
v = alpha * (c - cmax)
# To avoid positive due cmax from c? no
w = np.exp(v)
wsum = w.sum()
if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <=0:
   w = np.zeros_like(c); imax=int(np.argmax(c)); w[imax]=1.0; wsum=1.0
f = cmax + np.log(wsum)/alpha
w /= wsum
Fw = np.fft.rfft(w,N)
Fwr = phase * np.conj(Fw)
S1 = np.fft.irfft(Fwr * H, N)[n-1:n-1+n]
S2 = np.fft.irfft(Fw * H, N)[n-1:n-1+n]
pref = np.empty(2*n, dtype=float); pref[0]=0; np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:])
A = pref[starts+n] - pref[starts]
grad = dx*(A - S1 - S2)
diff = x.sum() - target
f += 0.5*mu*diff*diff
grad += mu*diff
# optional clip grad if nonfinite
if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
   grad = np.zeros_like(x)
   f = float('inf')? But minimize handles? Return large.
return float(f), grad
```
Should we include `vmax` if v has tiny positive due cmax computed before clipping? If cmax = max, all v≤0. Good.
If alpha large and wsum exactly 1 for max count, f=cmax. log(1)=0.

But when `w` all zeros due underflow? v for max =0, exp(0)=1, so wsum>0.

Now `sqp_optimize` with ctx? It needs exact c and gradients. We'll implement using exact for n <= maybe 3000. For active selection, use exact c.
```
def sqp_optimize(h,n,deadline,max_iter=200,rho=0.02,max_active=60):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       c=c_vec_exact(h,n) # full length
       cmax=c.max()
       tol=max(1e-9, 1e-5*cmax)
       active=np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
       if active.size > max_active:
          order=np.argsort(-c[active])
          active=active[order[:max_active]]
       nvar=n+1
       c_obj=np.zeros(nvar); c_obj[-1]=1.0
       A_ub=np.zeros((active.size,nvar))
       b_ub=np.zeros(active.size)
       for r,idx in enumerate(active):
          g=shift_grad_single(h,n,int(idx))
          A_ub[r,:n]=g
          A_ub[r,n]=-1.0
          b_ub[r]=-c[idx]
       bounds=[]
       for i,hi in enumerate(h):
          lo = max(-rho, -hi)
          hi_b = min(rho, 1.0 - hi)
          if lo > hi_b:
             lo = hi_b = 0.0
          bounds.append((lo,hi_b))
       bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
       A_eq=np.zeros((1,nvar)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
       b_eq=np.zeros(1)
       try:
          res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=b_eq,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
       except Exception:
          res=None
       if res is None or not res.success:
          rho *= 0.5
          no_improve +=1
          if rho < 1e-6 or no_improve > 20: break
          continue
       delta=res.x[:n]
       # if delta all zero, maybe subgradient
       if np.max(np.abs(delta)) < 1e-12:
           # try small random? break
           no_improve +=1
           rho*=0.5
           if rho<1e-6 or no_improve>5: break
           continue
       accepted=False
       for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
           h_try = h + scale*delta
           # due LP bounds, h_try within [0,1] up to tol; clip
           np.clip(h_try,0.0,1.0,out=h_try)
           # sum should be target; adjust if needed
           s = h_try.sum()
           diff = 0.5*n - s
           if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
               # distribute among interior
               mask = (h_try > 1e-10) & (h_try < 1.0 - 1e-10)
               if mask.any():
                   h_try[mask] += diff / mask.sum()
                   np.clip(h_try,0.0,1.0,out=h_try)
               else:
                   h_try = project_feasible(h_try,n)
           c_try = exact_c5(h_try,n)
           if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
               best_c = c_try; best_h = h_try.copy(); h = h_try; accepted=True
               no_improve=0
               if scale == 1.0 and rho < 0.12:
                   rho = min(rho*1.2, 0.12)
               break
       if not accepted:
           no_improve +=1
           rho *= 0.5
           if rho < 1e-6 or no_improve > 25: break
    return best_h,best_c
```

Upsampling function:
```
def resample_h(h, n):
    n0=len(h)
    if n0 == n: return h.copy()
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n0)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    return np.interp(x1,x0,h)
```

Spectral perturb function top-level:
```
def spectral_perturb(h,n,seed,amp=0.02,bandwidth=20):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    F=np.fft.rfft(h,n)
    size=len(F)
    k=np.arange(size, dtype=float)
    mask=np.exp(-(k/float(max(1,bandwidth)))**2)
    # keep DC exact? If perturb, project fixes sum.
    noise=rng.standard_normal(size).astype(complex) + 1j*rng.standard_normal(size).astype(complex)
    noise[0]=0.0
    F2=F + amp*noise*mask
    h2=np.fft.irfft(F2,n)
    return project_feasible(h2,n)
```
Need `np.random.default_rng` available (numpy). Yes.
Could use `np.random.seed` older. Both allowed. Use default_rng deterministic.

Maybe the exact objective's max includes `dx` and `h` step function. If we choose n very large, c values at integer shifts approximate continuous. But there is a known concept: minimizing max_k ∫ h(x)(1-h(x+k)) is equivalent to finding a function with low "upper right autocorrelation". The optimum might be achieved by a function with h(x)+h(x+1)=1? We saw antisymmetry causes one-direction high at lag1. But if h is neither too high nor low? Let's derive necessary condition for lag1: For x∈[0,1], c_+(1)=∫ h(x)(1-h(x+1)) dx. c_-(1)=∫ h(x+1)(1-h(x)) dx. Sum = ∫ [h(1-h') + h'(1-h)] dx = ∫ [h + h' -2hh']? But h,h'∈[0,1]. If h'=1-h, sum=∫ h^2+(1-h)^2. Minimum constant gives 0.5. If not, can be lower? At each x, f(a,b)=a(1-b)+b(1-a)=a+b-2ab. For fixed sum? Min over a,b maybe? If a=b=0.5, f=0.5? If a=0,b=0, f=0; but total constraints. Since c_+(1)+c_-(1) = ∫(a+b-2ab). For pair sum s, product max at s/2, f = s -2p. For fixed s, max product reduces f. Since total areas of h on [0,1] and [1,2] maybe? Let A=∫0^1 h, B=∫1^2 h, total A+B=1. Can we make c_+(1)+c_-(1) small by making h and h' similarly valued? If h(x+1)≈h(x), f≈2h(1-h), average maybe? For period h, sum=2∫h(1-h) over length1. To make small, h near 0/1 with area 0.5 per period -> 2*0? Actually binary period with h pattern area0.5 gives h(1-h)=0, sum0. But other lags. For lag1, good if h roughly periodic. For lag0, good if h roughly binary. So binary periodic may handle both lag0 and lag1. But mid lags? Earlier binary interval period gave max 0.5. Maybe a binary periodic pattern with low cross-correlation (between A and complement) within period can lower. This is like finding binary sequence of length m with half ones minimizing max mismatch at all cyclic shifts, but with weight of shift? For period1 over [0,2] with pattern length m repeated twice, for small k within period, c over length≈2 includes two contributions; might be constrained. Maybe a good binary pattern (e.g., Golay complementary?) could achieve 0.38. Parent likely binary-ish? The current record 0.38085 maybe from binary sequence. If so, direct smooth L-BFGS may not preserve binary; but SQP/local can improve.

If binary, h values 0/1, sum n/2. Could use exact discrete local search over bit flips (swap a 1 and 0 to maintain sum) to reduce max mismatch. This could be effective and different from smooth. Let's explore.

For binary h, c_k counts mismatches 10 for positive, 01 for negative. A flip (swap 1 at i and 0 at j) changes counts. Could local search find lower than smooth? The previous targeted local search did continuous swaps of small step, not bit flips. If optimum is binary, bit local search might improve. Implementation of binary local search:
- Given h continuous, produce binary h_b: choose n/2 largest values as 1, rest 0 (ensures area). This is a projection to binary with same order. Evaluate C. If better, keep.
- Local search: while time, select active shifts. For each candidate move (swap one 1 and one zero), compute change in C quickly? Hard. But we can try random swaps and compute exact c O(n^2) each too slow for n=500? 250k per evaluation; 1000 eval =250M, maybe okay? For n=500, maybe. For n=1024, 1M*100=100M. Could use if time. Guided binary search:
For current c vector, active indices with high c. For positive lag d (index c+d), c_d counts pairs (i,i+d) with h_i=1,h_{i+d}=0. To reduce c_d, we can swap a 1 at i with a 0 at i+d (making both? If swap: h_i 1->0, h_{i+d}0->1, then that specific pair becomes 0-1, reducing directional c_d by 1, but may affect other lags). For negative active, similar. So candidate swaps are (i, j=i±d) where h_i=1, h_j=0 (for positive lag) or h_j=1,h_i=0 for negative? Specifically if active positive lag d (shift right count h_i(1-h_{i+d})), a 1 at i and 0 at i+d; swapping makes h_i=0,h_{i+d}=1, reducing that pair. For active negative lag -e (index c-e), c counts h_{i+e}(1-h_i), pairs 1 at i+e and 0 at i; swapping i,i+e reduces. So candidate swap positions are endpoints of active mismatched pairs. We can collect a set of candidate pairs from active lags and evaluate.

This guided local search could be effective. Implement for n<=512 or 1024 with limited candidates.

Delta update for binary swap exactly:
Given binary array h, c vector length 2n-1 (corr h,1-h). For swap p (1->0) and q (0->1). We can compute new c by considering old and new contributions for all lags affected by p or q. We can do O(n) vectorized by constructing positions.
For a single index change of h at p from 1 to 0 and 1-h from 0 to1, but we have two changes. Could compute new c from old by removing old terms involving p or q and adding new? Since after swap, only values at p and q changed. For each lag d, old contribution is sum over i of old_h[i]*(1-old_h[i+d]). New = sum new_h[i]*(1-new_h[i+d]). The difference only nonzero for i where i∈{p,q} or i+d∈{p,q}. We can compute new by setting `hp = h.copy(); hp[p]=0; hp[q]=1; h1p=1-hp` and recompute full c? That's O(n^2). But to compute delta, we can for each lag d in affected range compute old contributions for the few i values and new contributions.
Specifically, for a lag d, affected i values are those in set `{p, q, p-d, q-d}` within [0,n-1] and i+d valid. The old contribution from affected i can be computed directly from old h, new from new h. Difference = sum_new_contrib - sum_old_contrib (but if affected i duplicate avoid double count). We can vectorize over d arrays using boolean masks and h values at shifted indices.

Compute components explicitly:
A = -corr(h,δ). corr(h,δ)_d = h_{p-d}*(-1)? Wait δ_i? corr(h,δ)=Σ h_i δ_{i+d}. Nonzero if i+d=p (δ=-1) or q (δ=+1).

This guided binary local search is great. Let's implement `binary_local_search(h, n, deadline, max_candidates_per_iter=...)`.

Procedure:
1. Convert to balanced binary b:
```
b = np.zeros(n); target=n//2
# if h already binary with target ones? Use sorted
if np.all((h==0)|(h==1)) and h.sum()==target: b=h.astype(int)
else:
   idx=np.argpartition(-h, target-1)[:target] if target>0; b[idx]=1
```
If target=0? n≥2. For exact target n/2, even.
2. If initial C_b >= current best? We can still local search maybe find better below best. But if C_b much worse, skip? Could still. Set `c_b=exact_c_vec(b,n)`, `best_c_b=c_b.max()`. If best_c_b < best_c, update global.
3. If time small, return.
4. We'll perform local search while time < deadline:
   - c = exact_c_vec(b,n) maybe maintain after accepted. We can maintain c vector from previous exact to avoid recompute? At start compute. After accepting a swap, we can update c via delta exactly, so no need recomputing. We can trust but maybe recompute every few steps for sanity. Use maintained c.
   - Find active lags: top indices with c close to max. `tol=max(1e-12, 1e-4*c_max)`. active = c >= cmax - tol, if >20 take top 20.
   - Generate candidate moves from active lags:
      ones = np.flatnonzero(b), zeros = np.flatnonzero(1-b). If one/zero empty break.
      For each idx in active:
         d = idx - (n-1)
         if d > 0:
             # positive lag d: mismatched pairs i with b[i]=1, b[i+d]=0. Candidate swap (i, i+d).
             # To avoid too many, sample up to K per lag.
             mask = (b[:n-d]==1) & (b[d:]==0)
             positions = np.flatnonzero(mask)
             if count > K: positions = rng.choice(positions, K, replace=False)
             for i in positions: candidates.add((int(i), int(i+d)))
         elif d < 0:
             e=-d
             # negative lag -e: c = ∑ h_{i+e}(1-h_i); mismatches pair i with b[i+e]=1, b[i]=0; swap (i+e, i)
             mask = (b[e:]==1) & (b[:n-e]==0)
             positions = np.flatnonzero(mask) # i
             for i: candidates.add((int(i+e), int(i)))
         else:
             # c0 zero for binary; skip or random
      - Also add random swaps: e.g., 20 random pairs from ones/zeros to escape.
      - For each candidate (p,q) (p should be 1, q 0; if not swap roles), evaluate delta and max. Keep best candidate with newmax < current_max - 1e-12 (or below best_c). If multiple, choose lowest newmax.
      - If found accept: update b[p]=0,b[q]=1; c += delta; max = newmax; no_improve=0; maybe if max < global_best update best. Else no_improve++.
      - If no improve over candidates: perhaps try larger random sample (all random) or break after several no improvements.
   - Limit candidate count to avoid time: compute delta O(n), max O(n). For n=512, evaluating 200 candidates = 200*1k ~200k ops (fast). 1000 okay. For n=1024, 200*2k ~400k. Good. But Python overhead per candidate slices maybe. Limit 100 active candidates + 50 random.

   - For random swaps, choose `p = ones[rng.integers(len(ones))]`, `q=zeros[...]`.
   - The swap delta formula assumes p is 1 ->0 and q 0 ->1. If candidate has p=0,q=1, swap variables. Ensure.

Candidate delta function top-level to avoid passing many arrays:
```
def binary_swap_delta(c? no, h, p, q, n, c_center, h_rev, g):
   L=2*n-1
   delta=np.zeros(L)
   delta[p:p+n] += h_rev
   delta[c_center-p:c_center-p+n] -= g
   delta[q:q+n] -= h_rev
   delta[c_center-q:c_center-q+n] += g
   delta[c_center] -= 2
   d = q-p
   delta[c_center+d] += 1
   delta[c_center-d] += 1
   return delta
```
But h_rev and g change after accepted. We can pass and maintain. For index `c_center-d` maybe same as c_center+d if d=0 (p=q impossible). If d could be n? p,q within [0,n-1], |d|≤n-1, index valid 0..2n-2.

Implement `binary_local_search(h, n, deadline, external_best_c)`:
- `dx=2/n`, target=n//2.
- Build b balanced.
- c_raw = np.correlate(b, 1-b, 'full').astype(float)
- `current_raw = c_raw.max()`; `current_c5=current_raw*dx`.
- `best_raw=current_raw`; `best_b=b.copy()`
- If current_c5 >= external_best_c + 0.005, maybe local search could still cross, but if not, skip? We can still for a limited time. If current much worse (e.g., 0.45) and target 0.38, random search unlikely. But guided may. Keep if time.
- Prepare `h_rev = b[::-1].astype(float)`, `g = 1.0 - b`.
- rng = default_rng(seed from external? Use seed param).
- while time < deadline:
   cmax = float(c_raw.max())
   if cmax*dx < external_best_c - 1e-12: update best_b? Actually maintain best_b within function each accept.
   active_tol = max(1e-12, 1e-4*cmax)
   active_idx = np.where(c_raw >= cmax - active_tol)[0]
   if active_idx.size > 20: active_idx = active_idx[np.argsort(-c_raw[active_idx])[:20]]
   candidates = []
   ones = np.flatnonzero(b); zeros = np.flatnonzero(1-b)
   for idx in active_idx:
       d = int(idx - (n-1))
       if d>0:
           if d >= n: break
           mask = (b[:n-d]==1) & (b[d:]==0)
           pos = np.flatnonzero(mask)
           if pos.size:
              if pos.size > 8: pos = pos[rng.choice(pos.size, 8, replace=False)]
              for i in pos: candidates.append((int(i), int(i+d)))
       elif d<0:
           e=-d
           mask=(b[e:]==1)&(b[:n-e]==0)
           pos=np.flatnonzero(mask)
           if pos.size:
              if pos.size>8: pos=pos[rng.choice(pos.size,8,False)]
              for i in pos: candidates.append((int(i+e), int(i)))
   # random
   if len(ones)>0 and len(zeros)>0:
       rr = rng.integers(0, len(ones), size=min(30, len(ones)*len(zeros)))
       rz = rng.integers(0, len(zeros), size=rr.size)
       for a,bidx in zip(rr,rz): candidates.append((int(ones[a]), int(zeros[bidx])))
   # cap candidates 150: if len>150: candidates=candidates[:150]
   accepted=False
   for p,q in candidates:
       if p==q: continue
       if b[p]==0 and b[q]==1: p,q=q,p
       if b[p]!=1 or b[q]!=0: continue
       delta = np.zeros(L)
       delta[p:p+n] += h_rev
       delta[c_center-p:c_center-p+n] -= g
       delta[q:q+n] -= h_rev
       delta[c_center-q:c_center-q+n] += g
       delta[c_center] -= 2
       diff = q-p
       delta[c_center+diff] += 1
       delta[c_center-diff] += 1
       # newmax: we can update c_raw copy
       # To avoid allocation, maybe use tmp = c_raw + delta
       try:
          new_raw = float((c_raw + delta).max())
       except: break
       if new_raw + 1e-12 < cmax:
           # accept
           b[p]=0; b[q]=1
           c_raw = c_raw + delta # could reuse tmp? We computed max but not stored. Recreate or assign.
           c_raw[c_raw<0 and >-1e-9]=0? due float.
           cmax = new_raw
           h_rev = b[::-1].astype(float)
           g = 1.0 - b
           # maybe if cmax improves best raw, update best_b
           if cmax*dx < best_raw*dx - 1e-14:
               best_raw=cmax; best_b=b.copy()
           accepted=True
           break
   if not accepted:
       no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 10: break
       # also random broader? continue
   # optional every 20 accepted recompute c_raw to avoid drift
   if accepted and (accept_count % 50 == 0): c_raw = np.correlate(b,1-b,'full').astype(float)
- Return best_b, best_raw*dx.
```

    if ctx is not None and time.time() < deadline - 30:

Now, if c_new exact is less than best but h_new maybe not the h that gave c_new? We compute exact on h_new. Good.

Now, in run, after n0 L-BFGS and SQP, if best_n changed? optimize_lbfgs returns h length same; best_n remains. If SQP returns h same length. Good.

When upsample to target_n, if target_n <= best_n maybe skip to avoid downsampling unless target_n 512 and best_n <512? Use:
```

    if tn <= best_n: continue
```
Since parent 500, run 512, then best_n might remain 500 (lower c). Next tn=1024. If 512 improved and best_n=512, next 1024. If best_n=1024, next 2048. Good. If 500 still best, still try 1024. But we skip only if tn <= best_n. If best_n=500, 512 >500. Good.

For binary search, choose `n_bin = best_n if best_n <= 1024 else 1024`, and if n_bin < 256 maybe 256? Parent 500. If best_n=2048, resample to 1024. If binary at lower n improves c5 (dx different), update best_n to n_bin. Good.

```

   ctx=build_context(best_n)
   h_ref = best_h.astype(float) if binary else best_h
   h_r,c_r=optimize_lbfgs(h_ref,best_n,ctx,min(time.time()+60,deadline-20),[500,2000,8000],20,250)
   if c_r<best: update
```
But if best_h binary values 0/1, L-BFGS with bounds can smooth. Good.

Now, possible issue with `_STOP_DEADLINE` global: If `optimize_lbfgs` called with a deadline that is later than next? We set to passed deadline. Callback uses current global. If two calls nested? no.

    return None
```

Candidate evaluation in binary search uses `delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)` each candidate. `h_rev` and `g` float arrays. Slices with `delta[p:p+n] += h_rev`: If p+n <= L, yes. The h_rev length n. Good.
`delta[c_center - p : c_center - p + n] -= g`: c_center-p could be negative? p max n-1 => c-p=0; min c-p? p=0 => c, +n =2n-1 = L. Good.
`delta[q:q+n] -= h_rev`; `delta[c_center - q : c_center-q+n] += g`.
D: `delta[c_center] -= 2.0`; `dd = q-p`; `delta[c_center+dd] += 1.0`; `delta[c_center-dd] += 1.0`. If dd=0, would add 2 at center but p!=q. If dd positive or negative valid. Note if dd positive, c+dd and c-dd distinct. If dd negative, same terms but order; valid. If `c_center + dd` index duplicate if dd=0 only. Good.

Now, possible hidden problem: The objective uses `max(np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode="full") * dx)`. The correlation partial sums for shifts near edges have smaller overlap; our c vector includes those. For binary delta formula, D and slices produce changes for all lags including edges correct.

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
import time

_STOP_DEADLINE = 0.0


def _lbfgs_callback(xk):
    if time.time() > _STOP_DEADLINE:
        return True
    return None


def build_context(n):
    n = int(n)
    m = max(1, 3 * n - 2)
    N = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    ones_fft = np.fft.rfft(np.ones(n, dtype=float), N)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    phase = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * np.arange(ones_fft.size, dtype=float) * (L - 1) / float(N))
    starts = np.arange(n - 1, -1, -1, dtype=np.intp)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    target = 0.5 * int(n)
    return (n, N, ones_fft, phase, starts, dx, target)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    s = float(h.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-14:
        return h

    if abs(s - target) < 1e-8:
        diff = target - s
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-12)
        else:
            mask = h > (1e-12)
        if mask.any():
            h[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
        return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    lo = float(h.min()) - 1.0 - 1e-12
    hi = float(h.max()) + 1.0 + 1e-12
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        p = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    p = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(p.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        mask = (p > 1e-12) & (p < (1.0 - 1e-12))
        if mask.any():
            p[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
        else:
            if diff > 0:
                idx = np.argsort(1.0 - p)[:max(1, int(abs(diff)) + 2)]
                for ii in idx:
                    room = 1.0 - p[ii]
                    add = min(room, max(0.0, diff))
                    p[ii] += add
                    diff -= add
                    if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
                        break
            else:
                idx = np.argsort(p)[:max(1, int(-diff) + 2)]
                for ii in idx:
                    room = p[ii]
                    sub = min(room, max(0.0, -diff))
                    p[ii] -= sub
                    diff += sub
                    if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
                        break
    return np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0)


def exact_c5(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def c_vec_exact(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx


def c_vec_fft(h, ctx):
    n, N, ones_fft, phase, starts, dx, target = ctx
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    G = ones_fft - H
    I = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(G), N)
    c = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    c[:n] = I[n - 1:0:-1]
    c[n:] = I[N - 1:N - n:-1]
    c *= dx
    return c


def smooth_fun_and_grad(x, ctx, alpha, mu):
    n, N, ones_fft, phase, starts, dx, target = ctx
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)

    H = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    G = ones_fft - H
    I = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(G), N)

    c = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    c[:n] = I[n - 1:0:-1]
    c[n:] = I[N - 1:N - n:-1]
    c *= dx

    cmax = float(c.max())
    v = alpha * (c - cmax)
    w = np.exp(v)
    wsum = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        wsum = 1.0

    f = cmax + np.log(wsum) / alpha
    w /= wsum

    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    Fwr = phase * np.conj(Fw)

    S1 = np.fft.irfft(Fwr * H, N)[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    S2 = np.fft.irfft(Fw * H, N)[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

    pref = np.empty(2 * n, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:])
    A = pref[starts + n] - pref[starts]

    grad = dx * (A - S1 - S2)

    diff = float(x.sum()) - target
    f += 0.5 * mu * diff * diff
    grad += mu * diff

    if not np.isfinite(f) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e8, np.zeros_like(x)
    return float(f), grad


def optimize_lbfgs(h, n, ctx, deadline, alphas=None, mu=10.0, maxiter=700):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)

    if alphas is None:
        if n <= 700:
            alphas = [20, 80, 300, 1000, 4000, 10000]
        elif n <= 1300:
            alphas = [50, 200, 800, 3000, 10000]
        elif n <= 2600:
            alphas = [100, 500, 2000, 8000]
        else:
            alphas = [200, 1000, 5000]

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    start = h.copy()

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        _STOP_DEADLINE = deadline - 1.0
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_fun_and_grad,
                start,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                args=(ctx, alpha, mu),
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": max(10, 20 * maxiter),
                    "ftol": 1e-18,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxls": 50,
                    "callback": _lbfgs_callback,
                },
            )
            x_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float) if res.x is not None else start
        except Exception:
            continue

        h_new = project_feasible(x_new, n)
        c_new = exact_c5(h_new, n)
        if c_new < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = c_new
            best_h = h_new.copy()
        if c_new <= best_c + 1e-7:
            start = h_new
        else:
            start = best_h

    _STOP_DEADLINE = time.time() + 1.0
    return best_h, best_c


def shift_grad_single(h, n, idx):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
    if k >= 0:
        nv = n - k
        grad[0:nv] += (1.0 - h[k:n]) * dx
        if nv > 0:
            grad[k:n] += (-h[0:nv]) * dx
    else:
        kk = -k
        nv = n - kk
        grad[kk:n] += (1.0 - h[0:nv]) * dx
        if nv > 0:
            grad[0:nv] += (-h[kk:n]) * dx
    return grad


def sqp_optimize(h, n, deadline, max_iter=80, rho=0.03, max_active=50):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = c_vec_exact(h, n)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        tol = max(1e-9, 1e-5 * cmax)
        active = np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            break
        if active.size > max_active:
            order = np.argsort(-c[active])
            active = active[order[:max_active]]

        nvar = n + 1
        c_obj = np.zeros(nvar)
        c_obj[-1] = 1.0
        A_ub = np.zeros((active.size, nvar))
        b_ub = np.zeros(active.size)

        for r, idx in enumerate(active):
            g = shift_grad_single(h, n, int(idx))
            A_ub[r, 0:n] = g
            A_ub[r, n] = -1.0
            b_ub[r] = -c[idx]

        lo = np.maximum(-rho, -h)
        hi = np.minimum(rho, 1.0 - h)
        bad = lo > hi
        if bad.any():
            lo[bad] = 0.0
            hi[bad] = 0.0
        bounds = [(float(lo[i]), float(hi[i])) for i in range(n)]
        bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))

        A_eq = np.zeros((1, nvar))
        A_eq[0, 0:n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.zeros(1)

        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
            if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None:
                raise RuntimeError("linprog failed")
            delta = np.asarray(res.x[0:n], dtype=float)
        except Exception:
            rho *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if rho < 1e-7 or no_improve > 25:
                break
            continue

        if float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) < 1e-12:
            rho *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if rho < 1e-7 or no_improve > 8:
                break
            continue

        accepted = False
        for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            h_try = h + scale * delta
            np.clip(h_try, 0.0, 1.0, out=h_try)
            s = float(h_try.sum())
            diff = 0.5 * n - s
            if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
                mask = (h_try > 1e-12) & (h_try < 1.0 - 1e-12)
                if mask.any():
                    h_try[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
                    np.clip(h_try, 0.0, 1.0, out=h_try)
                else:
                    h_try = project_feasible(h_try, n)

            c_try = exact_c5(h_try, n)
            if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c_try
                best_h = h_try.copy()
                h = h_try
                accepted = True
                no_improve = 0
                if scale == 1.0 and rho < 0.12:
                    rho = min(0.12, rho * 1.2)
                break

        if not accepted:
            rho *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if rho < 1e-7 or no_improve > 25:
                break

    return best_h, best_c


def resample_h(h, n):
    n0 = int(len(h))
    n = int(n)
    if n0 == n:
        return np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    x0 = (np.arange(n0, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n0)
    x1 = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, np.asarray(h, dtype=float))


def random_fourier_h(n, seed, maxfreq=30, amp=0.2):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = int(n)
    F = np.zeros(n // 2 + 1, dtype=complex)
    F[0] = 0.5 * n
    m = min(maxfreq, n // 2)
    for k in range(1, m + 1):
        a = amp / np.sqrt(float(k + 1))
        theta = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        if k == n // 2:
            F[k] = float(a * np.cos(theta))
        else:
            F[k] = a * np.exp(1j * theta)
    h = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def spectral_perturb(h, n, seed, amp=0.015, bandwidth=18):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = int(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n)
    k = np.arange(len(F), dtype=float)
    mask = np.exp(-0.25 * (k / float(max(1, bandwidth))) ** 2)
    noise = (
        rng.standard_normal(len(F)).astype(complex)
        + 1j * rng.standard_normal(len(F)).astype(complex)
    )
    noise[0] = 0.0
    F2 = F + amp * noise * mask
    h2 = np.fft.irfft(F2, n)
    return project_feasible(h2, n)


def binary_local_search(h, n, deadline, seed, external_best_c):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 8:
        return h, external_best_c

    target = n // 2
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    if (
        np.all((h <= 1e-12) | (h >= 1.0 - 1e-12))
        and abs(float(h.sum()) - target) < 1e-8
    ):
        b = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    else:
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        if target > 0:
            idx = np.argpartition(-h, target - 1)[:target]
            b[idx] = 1.0

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(b, 1.0 - b, mode="full").astype(float)
    cur_raw = float(c.max())
    cur_c5 = cur_raw * dx

    best_b = b.copy()
    best_raw = cur_raw

    if cur_c5 < external_best_c:
        best_b = b.copy()
        best_raw = cur_raw

    if time.time() > deadline:
        return project_feasible(best_b, n), best_raw * dx

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    h_rev = b[::-1].astype(float)
    g = 1.0 - b
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c_center = n - 1
    no_improve = 0
    accept_since_recenter = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if accept_since_recenter >= 60:
            c = np.correlate(b, 1.0 - b, mode="full").astype(float)
            h_rev = b[::-1].astype(float)
            g = 1.0 - b
            accept_since_recenter = 0

        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax * dx < best_raw * dx - 1e-14:
            best_raw = cmax
            best_b = b.copy()

        tol = max(1e-12, 1e-4 * cmax)
        active = np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
        if active.size > 20:
            order = np.argsort(-c[active])
            active = active[order[:20]]
        if active.size == 0:
            break

        ones = np.flatnonzero(b)
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(1.0 - b)
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break

        candidates = []
        for idx in active:
            d = int(idx - c_center)
            if d > 0:
                if d >= n:
                    continue
                mask = (b[0:n - d] == 1.0) & (b[d:] == 0.0)
                pos = np.flatnonzero(mask)
                if pos.size == 0:
                    continue
                if pos.size > 8:
                    pos = pos[rng.choice(pos.size, 8, replace=False)]
                for ii in pos:
                    candidates.append((int(ii), int(ii + d)))
            elif d < 0:
                e = -d
                if e >= n:
                    continue
                mask = (b[e:] == 1.0) & (b[0:n - e] == 0.0)
                pos = np.flatnonzero(mask)
                if pos.size == 0:
                    continue
                if pos.size > 8:
                    pos = pos[rng.choice(pos.size, 8, replace=False)]
                for ii in pos:
                    candidates.append((int(ii + e), int(ii)))

        nr = min(40, len(ones), len(zeros))
        if nr > 0:
            pi = rng.integers(0, len(ones), size=nr)
            qi = rng.integers(0, len(zeros), size=nr)
            for a, bb in zip(pi, qi):
                candidates.append((int(ones[a]), int(zeros[bb])))

        if len(candidates) > 150:
            candidates = candidates[:150]

        accepted = False
        for p, q in candidates:
            if p == q:
                continue
            if b[p] == 0.0 and b[q] == 1.0:
                p, q = q, p
            if b[p] != 1.0 or b[q] != 0.0:
                continue

            delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
            delta[p:p + n] += h_rev
            delta[c_center - p:c_center - p + n] -= g
            delta[q:q + n] -= h_rev
            delta[c_center - q:c_center - q + n] += g
            delta[c_center] -= 2.0
            dd = q - p
            delta[c_center + dd] += 1.0
            delta[c_center - dd] += 1.0

            new_c = c + delta
            new_raw = float(new_c.max())
            if new_raw + 1e-12 < cmax:
                b[p] = 0.0
                b[q] = 1.0
                c = new_c
                cmax = new_raw
                h_rev = b[::-1].astype(float)
                g = 1.0 - b
                accept_since_recenter += 1
                accepted = True
                if cmax * dx < best_raw * dx - 1e-14:
                    best_raw = cmax
                    best_b = b.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 18:
                break
        else:
            no_improve = 0

    best_b = project_feasible(best_b, n)
    c_final = exact_c5(best_b, n)
    return best_b, c_final


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 5.0)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h0 = random_fourier_h(512, seed, 40, 0.2)

    n0 = int(len(h0))
    if n0 > 2048:
        h0 = resample_h(h0, 2048)
        n0 = 2048

    best_h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_n = n0
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 30 and best_n <= 2500:
        ctx0 = build_context(best_n)
        end0 = min(t0 + 0.16 * budget_s, deadline - 0.45 * budget_s)
        if end0 > time.time() + 8:
            h1, c1 = optimize_lbfgs(
                best_h,
                best_n,
                ctx0,
                end0,
                alphas=[20, 80, 300, 1000, 4000, 10000],
                mu=10.0,
                maxiter=800,
            )
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, best_n

    if time.time() < deadline - 20 and best_n <= 2200:
        end_sq0 = min(t0 + 0.24 * budget_s, deadline - 0.35 * budget_s)
        if end_sq0 > time.time() + 5:
            h1, c1 = sqp_optimize(best_h, best_n, end_sq0, max_iter=70, rho=0.035)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h1, c1

   Trial_ns = (512, 1024, 2048)
    for tn in Trial_ns:
        if tn <= best_n:
            continue
        if time.time() > deadline - 110:
            break

        trial_h = resample_h(best_h, tn)
        ctx = build_context(tn)

        if tn <= 700:
            lb_sec = min(90.0, 0.12 * budget_s)
            sq_sec = min(45.0, 0.05 * budget_s)
            maxiter = 700
        elif tn <= 1300:
            lb_sec = min(210.0, 0.20 * budget_s)
            sq_sec = min(70.0, 0.07 * budget_s)
            maxiter = 520
        else:
            lb_sec = min(170.0, 0.16 * budget_s)
            sq_sec = min(25.0, 0.03 * budget_s)
            maxiter = 320

        lb_end = min(time.time() + lb_sec, deadline - 0.25 * budget_s - 20)
        if lb_end > time.time() + 8:
            h1, c1 = optimize_lbfgs(trial_h, tn, ctx, lb_end, mu=12.0, maxiter=maxiter)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, tn

        if tn <= 2200 and time.time() < deadline - 25:
            sq_end = min(time.time() + sq_sec, deadline - 12)
            if sq_end > time.time() + 4:
                h2, c2 = sqp_optimize(
                    best_h if best_n == tn else trial_h,
                    tn,
                    sq_end,
                    max_iter=45 if tn <= 700 else 30,
                    rho=0.025,
                )
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, tn

    if time.time() < deadline - 40 and best_n <= 2500:
        n_bin = best_n
        h_bin = resample_h(best_h, best_n)
        bin_end = min(time.time() + max(25.0, 0.06 * budget_s), deadline - 15)
        if bin_end > time.time() + 6:
            hb, cb = binary_local_search(h_bin, n_bin, bin_end, seed + 101, best_c)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hb, cb, n_bin

    if time.time() < deadline - 35 and best_n <= 1600:
        ctx_ref = build_context(best_n)
        ref_end = min(time.time() + max(20.0, 0.05 * budget_s), deadline - 6)
        if ref_end > time.time() + 5:
            h_ref = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
            h3, c3 = optimize_lbfgs(
                h_ref,
                best_n,
                ctx_ref,
                ref_end,
                alphas=[500, 2000, 8000],
                mu=18.0,
                maxiter=260,
            )
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h3, c3

            if best_n <= 1200 and time.time() < deadline - 10:
                h4, c4 = sqp_optimize(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2, max_iter=25, rho=0.02)
                if c4 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h4, c4

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
